TL;DR: 提出了一种解耦辐射度表示方法，通过2D surfels建模低频几何与视相关外观，结合紧凑纹理图集存储高频纹理，实现比3DGS快5倍且更稀疏的重建，支持消费级硬件上实时4K 60FPS渲染。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有3DGS扩展虽能通过哈希网格捕捉精细颜色细节，但片段渲染计算成本过高，需要更高效的表示方法。

Method: 1) 解耦辐射度表示：低频几何/视相关特征用2D surfels，高频纹理用视无关的空间哈希网格烘烤到紧凑纹理图集；2) 稀疏性增强优化：惩罚半透明度和逐基元衰减，激进剪枝无关surfels。

Result: 相比3DGS实现最高5倍加速，保持SOTA视觉质量，在消费级硬件上实现实时4K 60FPS渲染，且重建更稀疏。

Conclusion: 通过解耦低频与高频信息并利用几何稀疏性和高效GPU纹理映射，该方法在显著提升渲染速度的同时保持了高保真视觉效果。

Abstract: Recent extensions of 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) capture fine color details using hash-grid-based appearance parameterization but incur high computational cost during fragment rendering. We introduce a decoupled radiance representation that models low-frequency geometry and view dependent appearance features with 2D surfels while representing high-frequency textures via a view-independent spatial hash grid that is baked into a compact texture atlas. By including sparsity-enhancing optimizations that penalize semi-transparency and per-primitive falloff, our method aggressively prunes insignificant surfels and achieves significantly faster and sparser reconstructions than prior work. Exploiting geometric sparsity and efficient GPU texture mapping, our approach achieves up to a fivefold speedup over 3DGS while preserving state-of-the-art visual fidelity, enabling real-time 4K rendering at 60 FPS on consumer hardware.

</details>


### [72] [Towards Enhancing 3D Spatial Reasoning in Medical Multimodal Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13860)
*Zhuoyuan Fu,Zeshang Li,Yiqiong Zhang,Hangui Lin,Yan Shu,Yan Li,Binyang Li,Yaru Zhao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于切片级数据合成范式的结构化推理数据集构建方法，通过模拟放射科医生诊断流程，将3D医学图像理解分解为逐切片观察并合成可解释的思维链，在无需昂贵3D预训练的情况下显著提升了2D MLLM的立体空间推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM在3D医学影像理解上受限于高昂标注成本和数据集不透明性，且当前数据格式（刚性VQA对或非结构化临床报告）未能捕捉显式的临床推理过程。

Method: 提出切片级数据合成范式：借鉴放射科医生诊断流程，将复杂的3D阅片过程分解为细粒度的逐切片观察，再合成为可解释的思维链（CoT），并强制执行空间跟踪、多切片感知和鉴别排除等临床原则。

Result: 对标准2D预训练MLLM进行指令微调后，在多个3D医学基准上取得显著性能提升，展现出强空间推理能力，性能可与资源密集型的原生3D架构相媲美。

Conclusion: 这种以数据为中心的策略无需昂贵的3D特定预训练即可实现深度体积理解和高度可解释的临床推理，有效弥合了2D与3D方法之间的性能差距。

Abstract: While Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) have demonstrated remarkable success in 2D medical image understanding, their extension to 3D volumetric imaging remains hindered by prohibitive annotation costs and dataset opacity. Current data formats, predominantly consisting of rigid Visual Question Answering (VQA) pairs or unstructured final clinical reports, typically fail to capture explicit clinical reasoning. To address this limitation, we introduce a large-scale structured reasoning dataset constructed via a novel slice-wise data synthesis paradigm. Inspired by the genuine diagnostic workflow of radiologists, this paradigm models visual cognition by decomposing the complex 3D reading process, translating global clinical priors into fine-grained, per-slice observations that are subsequently synthesized into an interpretable Chain-of-Thought (CoT). Crucially, this synthesized reasoning framework enforces essential clinical principles: sequential spatial tracking, multi-slice spatial awareness for artifact mitigation, and differential exclusion. To validate this approach, we instruction-tune a standard 2D-pretrained MLLM baseline using the synthesized data to enhance its volumetric comprehension. Comprehensive evaluations across multiple 3D medical benchmarks demonstrate that our method yields significant performance improvements over the 2D baseline. Furthermore, the resulting model exhibits robust spatial reasoning capabilities and rivals resource-intensive native 3D architectures, effectively bridging the performance gap. Ultimately, this data-centric strategy unlocks deep volumetric understanding and highly interpretable clinical logic without requiring computationally expensive 3D-specific pre-training. The complete repository, including datasets and training workflows, is publicly available at https://github.com/2020420145009/hounsfield.

</details>


### [73] [Fine-Grained Vision-Language Pretraining with Organ-Conditioned Pattern Tokens for CT Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13892)
*Guoliang You,Xiaomeng Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出OCP-CT框架，通过器官条件化的模式-令牌对齐，实现CT影像与放射报告的视觉语言预训练，在零样本异常诊断中取得SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有CT视觉语言预训练方法存在局限性：全局扫描-报告对比掩盖了器官级别的异质证据，而直接的器官级对齐又因同一器官可呈现多种放射学表现而显得粗粒度。因此需要更细粒度的对齐单元——器官条件化的放射学模式。

Method: 提出OCP-CT框架，包含：（1）稳定的全局CT-报告对比分支；（2）器官模式接口：稀疏MoE根据潜在放射学模式路由图像和文本令牌，可学习插槽将路由令牌查询为连续模式令牌；（3）成对令牌对比：利用报告导出的临床相似性构建结构化软目标，对齐图像-文本模式令牌。

Result: 在CT-RATE和RAD-ChestCT基准上，零样本异常诊断平均AUROC分别达84.5%和69.9%，相比先前最强报告结果分别提升6.7和0.8个百分点。

Conclusion: OCP-CT通过器官条件化的模式令牌对齐，有效解决了CT视觉语言预训练中的异质性问题，显著提升了零样本诊断性能。

Abstract: Computed tomography (CT) vision-language pretraining from paired volumes and radiology reports is a scalable yet challenging task. Existing methods commonly adopt global scan-report contrast, which is scalable but obscures heterogeneous organ evidence. Meanwhile, direct organ-level alignment remains coarse, since the same anatomy can exhibit multiple distinct radiological appearances. Therefore, pretraining requires a finer alignment unit: the organ-conditioned radiological pattern. In this work, we propose OCP-CT, an organ-conditioned pattern-token alignment framework for CT vision-language pretraining. Specifically, OCP-CT preserves a stable global CT-report contrastive branch and introduces an organ pattern interface: sparse Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) routes image and text tokens according to latent radiological patterns, learnable slots query the routed tokens into continuous pattern tokens, and paired token contrast aligns image-text pattern tokens with structured soft targets built from report-derived clinical similarity. On the publicly available CT-RATE and RAD-ChestCT benchmarks, OCP-CT achieves average AUROCs of 84.5% and 69.9% for zero-shot abnormality diagnosis, respectively. Compared with the strongest prior reported results, these results yield absolute AUROC gains of 6.7 and 0.8 percentage points.

</details>


### [74] [The 2nd International StepUP Competition for Biometric Footstep Recognition: From Steps to Strides](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13905)
*Robyn Larracy,Anant Gupta,Gourav Gupta,Ethan Eddy,Maxime Devanne,Cyril Meyer,Jin-Chern Chiou,Yueh-Shan Lee,Zong-Han Lu,Aaron Tabor,Erik Scheme*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 第二届国际StepUP竞赛利用大规模步态数据集，聚焦三个挑战：泛化到未见用户、跨域鲁棒性（鞋类和速度变化）、左右脚融合。最佳方案（时空CNN+集成评分）达到8.00%等错误率，但识别未见个人鞋类仍是难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推进基于压力的足迹生物特征识别研究，通过标准化和具有挑战性的评估框架，解决实际应用中的泛化性、域偏移和足部融合问题。

Method: 使用StepUP-P150数据集（20万+高分辨率动态足迹，150人），设置三个挑战任务：未见用户小样本泛化、鞋类和速度域偏移鲁棒性、左右脚对融合。竞赛采用未公开测试集评估。

Result: 26个团队参赛，最佳系统（ArogyaPandit研究团队）达到8.00%等错误率，采用时空CNN+集成评分策略。

Conclusion: 时间模式利用和推理时归一化/校准策略有效，但识别未见个人鞋类仍具挑战，尤其存在相似特征干扰时。

Abstract: The International StepUP Competition Series was launched to advance research in pressure-based footstep biometrics through a standardized and challenging evaluation framework. Using the large-scale StepUP-P150 dataset (with more than 200,000 high-resolution dynamic footsteps from 150 individuals) and a previously unreleased test set, the 2nd edition of the competition addressed three key challenges: (1) generalization to unseen users with limited enrollment data, (2) robustness to domain shift caused by variations in footwear and walking speed and (3) effective fusion of paired left-right footsteps. While the first two challenges built on the inaugural competition, this edition introduced more extreme cross-domain conditions and moved beyond isolated footsteps to stride-level verification, enabling new opportunities for representation learning and inter-step information fusion. The competition attracted 26 registrants from academia and industry, with a best equal error rate of 8.00% achieved by the ArogyaPandit Research Team using a spatiotemporal CNN combined with an ensemble-based scoring strategy. The top solutions showcase the value of harnessing temporal patterns and of incorporating inference-time normalization and calibration strategies to improve scoring. However, the results also reveal that recognizing users in unseen personal footwear remains a challenge, especially in the presence of distractors with similar characteristics.

</details>


### [75] [Thresholded Cross-Attention for Reliable Intensity-Chromaticity Fusion in Low-Light Image Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13925)
*Yanyi Wu,Xu Zhang,Junkai Chen,Laibin Chang,Jiaqi Ma,Shi Chen,Linwei Zhu,Jianglei Di,Huan Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对低光图像增强中HVI空间的双流融合不可靠问题，提出了TCA-Net，用阈值化交叉注意力替代固定Top-K稀疏注意力，辅以相位引导傅里叶交互模块和解耦双流引导模块，在多个数据集上实现了竞争性的恢复精度、色彩保真度和紧凑参数量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HVI方法虽通过解耦亮度和色度来缓解色彩纠缠，但两流融合的可靠性被忽视；观察到跨流注意力的置信度具有强层依赖性，固定Top-K机制会丢弃有用信息或保留噪声，因此需要一种自适应的融合策略。

Method: 1) 阈值化交叉注意力(TCA)：用固定置信度阈值替代Top-K，保留数量随输入和层自适应；2) 相位引导傅里叶交互模块(PFIM)：在融合前为亮度流提供结构感知的亮度初始化；3) 解耦双流引导模块(DDSGM)：构建残差亮度特征抑制重建中的色度泄漏；4) 尺度感知一致性正则化(SACR)：在训练中增强尺度扰动下的结构鲁棒性。

Result: 在LOL-v1、LOL-v2、Sony-Total-Dark和LSRW-Huawei四个数据集上，TCA-Net实现了竞争性的恢复精度、更优的色彩保真度和紧凑的参数量。

Conclusion: 通过自适应保留高置信度跨流交互的阈值化注意力机制，结合结构感知亮度初始化和色度泄漏抑制模块，有效提升了HVI空间双流融合的可靠性，实现了低光图像增强在噪声抑制、色彩保真度和效率之间的更好平衡。

Abstract: Low-Light Image Enhancement (LLIE) requires a careful balance among noise suppression, color fidelity, and efficiency. Recent HVI-based methods alleviate color entanglement by decoupling intensity and chromaticity, yet how reliably the two streams are fused again is an overlooked factor that largely determines the final quality. We observe that the confidence of cross-stream attention is strongly layer-dependent, so the fixed-quota selection of Top-K sparse attention is mismatched to it, discarding informative dependencies in some layers while retaining noisy ones in others. Motivated by this observation, we propose TCA-Net, a network built around Thresholded Cross-Attention that targets reliable intensity-chromaticity fusion in the HVI space rather than introducing yet another color representation. At its core, TCA replaces the rigid Top-K quota with a fixed confidence threshold whose retained cardinality is input- and layer-adaptive, retaining only high-confidence cross-stream interactions while suppressing unreliable ones. Around this core, two complementary designs clean up the fusion before and after it: a Phase-guided Fourier Interaction Module provides a structure-aware brightness initialization for the intensity stream prior to fusion, and a Decoupled Dual-Stream Guidance Module constructs residual intensity features to suppress chromaticity leakage during reconstruction. A Scale-Aware Consistency Regularization further improves structural robustness under scale perturbations during training. Extensive experiments on LOL-v1, LOL-v2, Sony-Total-Dark, and LSRW-Huawei demonstrate that TCA-Net delivers competitive restoration accuracy, improved color fidelity, and a compact parameter size.

</details>


### [76] [Cyclone: Diffusion Model for Cycle-Consistent Weather Editing from Unpaired Driving Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13927)
*Thang-Anh-Quan Nguyen,Moussab Bennehar,Luis Guillermo Roldao Jimenez,Nathan Piasco,Dzmitry Tsishkou,Laurent Caraffa,Jean-Philippe Tarel,Roland Brémond*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了Cyclone，一个基于潜扩散的统一天气编辑框架，无需配对数据即可生成多种天气效果，提高自动驾驶感知系统的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法依赖合成数据增强或基于物理的任务特定模型，需要配对训练数据，难以生成真实天气效果或泛化到域外场景。

Method: 基于潜扩散模型，引入循环一致性约束和图像-文本模型知识，实现无需配对数据的多天气条件生成，并可蒸馏为视频扩散模型。

Result: 生成效果比现有基线更真实、保结构，并在多个下游驾驶感知任务中带来一致提升。

Conclusion: Cyclone是一种有效的去配对数据天气编辑框架，能提升感知鲁棒性，并支持扩展到时序一致的视频编辑。

Abstract: Reliable perception under diverse weather conditions remains a major challenge for autonomous driving systems. A common strategy to improve robustness is either to synthesize adverse weather conditions for training perception models or to apply weather-removal techniques to recover clean inputs. However, existing approaches typically rely on synthetic data augmentation or physics-based, task-specific models that require paired training data and often struggle to generate realistic weather effects or generalize robustly to out-of-domain scenarios. Toward this problem, we present Cyclone, a unified framework for weather editing based on latent diffusion, equipped with cycle-consistent constraints and knowledge from image-text models. Cyclone enables the generation of multiple weather conditions across diverse scenes while eliminating the need for paired data. Experimental results show that our approach produces more realistic, structure-preserving outputs than existing baselines and leads to consistent improvements across several downstream driving perception tasks. Furthermore, we demonstrate that Cyclone can be distilled to a video diffusion model for temporally consistent weather editing.

</details>


### [77] [SIVA-RL: Sensitivity-Invariance Visual Alignment for Multimodal Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13931)
*Cheng Tang,Junzhi Ning,Min Cen,Wei Li,Xinyi Zeng,Pinxian Zeng,Rongbin Li,Qiming Zhu,Yuqiang Li,Junjun He,Yirong Chen,Ming Hu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SIVA-RL框架，通过样本级别的基于结果的监督替代基于操作类型的正则化，利用PatchSwap构建局部干预，根据奖励下降幅度分配软路由权重，实现视觉-语言模型的视觉对齐，在9个多模态推理基准上显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉干预方法按干预类型分配监督，但相同操作在不同样本上产生不同效果，这种假设不成立，需要更细粒度的样本级别监督。

Method: 通过token对齐、距离约束的PatchSwap构建局部干预；使用冻结的审计策略对每对clean-intervention评分；根据奖励下降幅度分配软路由权重：大下降驱动敏感性对齐，小下降驱动不变性对齐，模糊样本降权。

Result: 在9个多模态推理基准上所有设置均提升，视觉依赖推理提升8.79个百分点，GRPO和DAPO两种主干上相对提升最高达14.9%。

Conclusion: SIVA-RL通过解耦干预构建与监督分配，有效提升了视觉-语言模型对视觉证据的grounding能力，在多种推理任务上取得一致性改进。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) drives multimodal reasoning, but answer-level correctness does not guarantee that a vision-language model grounds its predictions in visual evidence. Existing visual-intervention methods contrast policy behavior on original and modified images, yet assign supervision by the type of intervention rather than its observed effect. This assumption fails: identical operators produce heterogeneous outcomes across samples. We propose SIVA-RL, a Sensitivity-Invariance Visual Alignment framework that replaces operator-conditioned regularization with sample-wise, outcome-conditioned supervision. SIVA-RL constructs localized interventions through token-aligned, distance-constrained within-image PatchSwap. A frozen audit policy then scores each clean-intervention pair, and the observed reward drop becomes soft routing weights. Large-drop pairs drive sensitivity alignment, low-drop pairs drive clean-anchored invariance alignment, and ambiguous pairs are down-weighted. This design decouples intervention construction from supervision assignment and is compatible with both GRPO and DAPO backbones. Across nine multimodal reasoning benchmarks spanning mathematical, logical, and vision-dependent tasks, SIVA-RL improves 3B and 7B models over matched RL baselines in every setting. It yields an 8.79 percentage-point gain on vision-dependent reasoning and up to 14.9% relative overall improvement across all four GRPO- and DAPO-based configurations.

</details>


### [78] [A novel unsupervised machine learning strategy to handle multimodal cardiac PET/MRI data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13936)
*Brunnhilde Ponsi,Thomas Carlier,Lara Marteau,Aurélien Monnet,Thomas Eugène,Jean-Michel Serfaty,Nicolas Piriou,Hatem Necib*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于PET/MRI多参数数据的无监督聚类分析方法，通过对99名基因确诊的致心律失常性左心室心肌病患者的T1、T2、LGE和18F-FDG-PET图像进行两步聚类（先患者内超体素聚类，再患者间谱聚类），生成每个患者的“异常”评分及自动化的文本与牛眼图健康报告。该方法在重复嵌套交叉验证中与心脏影像医生评估的平衡准确率达0.76±0.04，并在167个数值体模上验证BA≥0.8，能够有效识别与纤维化或炎症相关的异常区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 致心律失常性左心室心肌病缺乏金标准诊断标准，现有影像学手段难以系统整合多模态PET/MRI数据来表征心肌异质性和疾病进展。

Method: 对99名基因确诊患者的T1、T2、LGE和18F-FDG-PET图像分别进行z-score标准化后求和为单一体积，采用两步聚类：首先对每个患者图像进行超体素聚类，然后通过谱聚类将超体素归为32个患者间组；为每个聚类和模态分配“异常”评分，用于生成自动化文本和牛眼图健康报告，并与心脏影像医师评估比较（重复嵌套交叉验证及167个数值体模验证）。

Result: 在99名患者上，重复嵌套交叉验证中平衡准确率为0.76±0.04；在167个数值体模上BA≥0.8；识别的异常聚类与医师视觉观察高度吻合，能区分不同程度的纤维化或炎症。

Conclusion: 该无监督聚类方法能够系统化处理多模态PET/MRI数据，有效表征致心律失常性左心室心肌病患者的心肌异质性，为缺乏金标准的疾病提供辅助诊断工具。

Abstract: Arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy is a genetic myocardial disease difficult to diagnose due to the lack of gold standard criteria. Simultaneous PET/MR imaging, combined with multiparametric quantitative analysis, could facilitate the identification of different profiles related to the phenotype and progression of cardiomyopathy. This preliminary study focuses on a methodological strategy for dealing with PET/MRI data, including inter-patient data linkage and regional analysis. Two-step clustering was applied to T1 and T2 maps, LGE, and 18F-FDG-PET images of 99 patients genetically diagnosed with arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy. Each patient's images were independently z-scored and summed into a single volume, which was clustered into supervoxels. Thirty-two inter-patient groups of supervoxels were obtained by spectral clustering. An "abnormality" score was assigned to each cluster and modality, and used to visualise abnormal regions likely associated with disease. They enabled the generation of automated textual and bullseye health reports for each patient, which were compared with cardiac imager assessments using balanced accuracy in repeated nested cross-validation. This approach was further validated on a larger cohort of 167 numerical phantoms. The reports generated by clustering accurately identified most of the cardiac physicians' observations (BA = 0.76 $\pm$ 0.04 in repeated nested cross-validation on patients, and BA $\ge$ 0.8 on phantoms). Furthermore, the identified abnormal clusters closely matched their visual observations, facilitating the identification of varying degrees of fibrosis or inflammation on the images. This approach enables a more systematic handling of multimodal PET/MRI data to characterise myocardial heterogeneity in arrhythmogenic left ventricular cardiomyopathy patients.

</details>


### [79] [Peak-End-Net: A Peak-End Rule Inspired Framework for Generalizable Video Aesthetic Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13941)
*Geng Li,Haiwen Li,Rui Chen,Jing Tang,Lei Sun,Xiangxiang Chu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文从心理学视角出发，提出基于峰终定律的轻量级视频美学评估框架Peak-End-Net，通过图像美学先验、美学节奏编码器和动态门控融合机制，在参数高效的前提下实现了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视频美学评估（VAA）远未得到充分探索，主要受限于大规模基准数据稀缺以及美学判断的主观性。现有方法忽视了人类感知心理机制，作者希望引入心理学中的"峰终定律"来更好地建模人类对视频美学的判断过程。

Method: 1) 利用预训练图像美学评估头提取帧级美学先验，作为美学显著性时刻的代理信号；2) 基于峰终定律的时间聚合机制；3) 美学节奏编码器建模视频美学随时间演化过程；4) 动态门控融合机制提升对分布偏移的鲁棒性。整体基于冻结的ViT，仅需少量可训练参数。

Result: 在VADB（域内评估）和DIVIDE-3K（跨域测试）两个基准上取得了SOTA性能，验证了心理建模对VAA的有效性。

Conclusion: 心理学的峰终定律可以有效指导视频美学评估任务，轻量级、可解释的框架设计在参数高效的同时取得了优异性能，为VAA研究提供了新范式。

Abstract: Video aesthetic assessment (VAA) aims to predict how aesthetically pleasing a video is, yet remains far less explored than other visual assessment tasks. Its progress is hindered not only by the scarcity of large-scale benchmarks, but also by the intrinsic subjectivity of aesthetic judgment, which is shaped by human perception. In this paper, we revisit VAA from a psychological perspective and propose \textit{Peak-End-Net}, a lightweight and interpretable framework inspired by the \textit{peak-end rule}, which suggests that people tend to judge a temporal experience mainly according to its salient moments and the ending. Building on this intuition, we first transfer knowledge from image aesthetic assessment (IAA) to VAA by introducing a pretrained IAA head to produce frame-wise aesthetic priors, which serve as surrogate signals for identifying aesthetically salient moments and guiding \textit{peak-end rule}-based temporal aggregation. To further capture how a video evolves aesthetically over time, we design an aesthetic rhythm encoder that models temporal progression beyond isolated moments. Additionally, we refine the overall assessment through a dynamic gated fusion mechanism to improve robustness under distribution shift. Our method is built on a frozen vision transformer (ViT) and requires only a small number of trainable parameters, making it scalable and parameter-efficient. Extensive experiments on two existing VAA benchmarks, including in-domain evaluation on VADB and cross-domain testing on DIVIDE-3K, demonstrate that our approach achieves state-of-the-art performance, affirming the value of psychologically grounded modeling for VAA. Our code and models are available at https://github.com/AMAP-ML/Peak-End-Net.

</details>


### [80] [PlumeQuant: Uncertainty-aware consistency assessment of methane plume masks and emission-rate estimates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13945)
*Parisa Masnadi Khiabani,Wolfgang Jentner,Alireza Rangrazjeddi,Michael C. Wimberly,Binbin Weng,David Ebert,Charles Nicholson*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文发现基于成像光谱仪的甲烷羽流产品中，相同的集成质量增强（IME）、羽流长度和排放率可对应多种不同但合理的羽流边界掩膜。作者提出PlumeQuant工具，通过遗传算法集合和透明掩膜生成方法，揭示了这种“等效性”问题，并提供了产品一致性诊断指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成像光谱仪生成的甲烷羽流产品中，羽流掩膜、IME、长度、排放率等标量参数在物理和算法上是相互关联的，但这些标量量值是否唯一约束了羽流边界尚不明确。作者希望揭示这种潜在的“等效性”（equifinality）问题，即相同标量值可能对应多种不同掩膜形态。

Method: 利用63个EMIT/Carbon Mapper羽流记录，通过遗传算法（GA）集合在给定IME和羽流长度条件下生成多种可能的掩膜。开发PlumeQuant工具，该工具可重新计算IME、长度、排放率及五分量不确定性，并评估四种掩膜表征：原始参考掩膜、Carbon Mapper模拟掩膜（CM-like）、GA集合掩膜以及专家编辑掩膜。CM-like掩膜在独立于场景的44羽流开发集上固定参数生成。

Result: 1) 目标一致掩膜集合中，高置信核心区域仅覆盖中位数13%的可能足迹包络，弱羽流和低重叠羽流歧义最大。2) CM-like掩膜与参考掩膜相比，IME中位数差异+0.72%，排放率+0.16%（平均绝对偏差6.98%），中位数IoU为0.843，不确定性尺度中位数比1.01。3) 留出集平均绝对误差：IME 7.6%，长度9.5%，排放率6.1%。

Conclusion: 已发表的标量参数（IME、长度、排放率）并不能唯一确定羽流边界，存在显著的等效性。PlumeQuant提供的一致性诊断可识别弱、偏移或模糊羽流，供专家进一步审查。但这些诊断并非独立验证，而是产品层面的质量控制工具。

Abstract: Imaging spectrometers increasingly distribute source-resolved methane plume products in which the plume mask, integrated mass enhancement (IME), plume length, emission rate, and uncertainty are physically and algorithmically linked. Using 63 EMIT-derived Carbon Mapper plume records from 27 scenes, we show that these published scalar quantities do not uniquely constrain the plume boundary: substantially different yet plausible masks reproduce the same IME, plume length, and emission rate. Genetic-algorithm (GA) ensembles conditioned on the published IME and plume length make this equifinality explicit: the high-confidence core selected by nearly all target-consistent masks covers a median of 13% of the plausible footprint envelope, and ambiguity is largest for weak, low-overlap plumes. The diagnostics come from PlumeQuant, which recomputes IME, plume length, emission rate, and five-term uncertainty from distributed product components under stated conventions and evaluates four mask representations: the distributed reference mask, a transparent Carbon Mapper-informed analogue (CM-like), the GA ensemble, and optional expert edits. The CM-like mask is generated per plume without access to the reference mask or published quantities, with settings fixed once on a scene-disjoint 44-plume development split. It reproduced published IME with +0.72% median difference and emission rate with +0.16% (6.98% mean absolute), reached 0.843 median intersection-over-union against the reference masks, and matched the published uncertainty scale (median ratio 1.01). Holdout mean absolute errors were 7.6% (IME), 9.5% (length), and 6.1% (rate). These are product-level consistency diagnostics, not independent validation. They flag weak, offset, or ambiguous plumes for expert review.

</details>


### [81] [CF-Net: Conflict Fusion with Speaker Normalisation and Certainty Weighting for Ambivalence/Hesitancy Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13976)
*Tung Hung Bui,Hong Hai Nguyen,Van Thong Huynh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出CF-Net多模态网络，通过显式建模跨模态不一致性（ConflictFusion模块）来检测视频中的矛盾/犹豫情绪，在BAH数据集上取得SOTA效果（验证集Macro F1 0.7155，测试集0.7364）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非约束视频中的矛盾与犹豫（AH）检测极具挑战性，因为目标信号本质模糊，通过微妙的跨模态不一致而非原型情感表达，现有方法难以有效捕捉这种细微信号。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的SigLIP2（视觉）、HuBERT（音频）、DistilBERT（文本）编码多模态流；2) 逐说话人归一化特征以减少身份泄漏；3) ConflictFusion模块显式计算成对跨模态不一致性；4) 训练采用确定权重焦点损失、流形混合、模态丢弃；5) 辅助确定性回归头利用模糊标注稳定学习。

Result: BAH验证集Macro F1=0.7155，私有挑战测试集Macro F1=0.7364（AP=0.7492）。

Conclusion: 显式建模跨模态不一致性可有效捕捉矛盾/犹豫情绪，消融实验验证各组件有效性，CF-Net为AH检测提供了一种新范式。

Abstract: Detecting ambivalence and hesitancy (AH) in unconstrained video is challenging because the target signal is inherently ambiguous and expressed through subtle cross-modal incongruence rather than prototypical affect. We present CF-Net, a deep multimodal network submitted to the 3rd Edition of the AH Video Recognition Challenge (ABAW 11th, ECCV 2026), targeting the BAH dataset. CF-Net encodes visual, audio, and transcript streams with frozen SigLIP2, HuBERT, and DistilBERT backbones, normalises backbone features per speaker to reduce identity leakage, and fuses them via a ConflictFusion module that explicitly computes pairwise cross-modal incongruence. Training combines certainty-weighted focal loss, manifold mixup, and modality dropout; an auxiliary certainty-regression head leverages ambiguity annotations to stabilise learning on genuinely borderline samples. CF-Net achieves a Macro F1 of 0.7155 on the BAH validation set and 0.7364 (AP = 0.7492) on the private challenge test set.

</details>


### [82] [Screening Is Effective for Visual Recognition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13983)
*Shunya Shimomura,Kazuhiro Hotta*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出VisionScreen，将语言模型中的Screening机制扩展至视觉识别领域，通过基于查询-键相似度的独立相关性评估，显式排除低相关图像块，替代传统ViT的softmax注意力机制。实验表明该方法在图像分类任务上优于传统ViT。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统ViT的self-attention对图像块赋予softmax归一化的相对权重，难以独立评估每个块的相关性，且无法显式排除背景或冗余块，导致不必要信息被引入特征聚合。

Method: 将Screening机制从语言模型迁移至视觉领域，提出VisionScreen：基于查询-键相似度对每个图像块进行独立相关性评估，通过阈值显式排除低相关块，并将这一绝对相关性估计扩展至二维空间域，使每个块仅聚合内容相关且空间相关的块。

Result: 在图像分类基准测试上，VisionScreen优于传统ViT模型。

Conclusion: Screening机制在视觉识别中同样有效，可作为基于softmax注意力的相对特征聚合的替代方案。

Abstract: Vision Transformer (ViT) has been widely used as a powerful framework for modeling global dependencies among image patches. However, its core component, self-attention assigns softmax-normalized relative weights to all patches, making it difficult to evaluate the relevance between patches independently. In visual recognition, images often contain many background or redundant patches, yet self-attention cannot explicitly reject such irrelevant patches, which may introduce unnecessary information into feature aggregation. To address this limitation, Screening has been proposed in the field of language modeling, where the relevance of each token is independently evaluated based on query-key similarity and low-relevance tokens are explicitly excluded through thresholding. In this work, we propose VisionScreen, a new vision model that extends Screening mechanism to visual recognition. VisionScreen treats image patches as tokens arranged on a two-dimensional grid and extends absolute relevance estimation based on query-key similarity to the two-dimensional spatial domain. This allows each patch to selectively aggregate only content-wise and spatially relevant patches without relying on competition among patches. Experiments on image classification benchmarks demonstrate that the proposed method outperforms conventional ViT. These results suggest that Screening can be effective for visual recognition, offering an alternative to relative feature aggregation based on softmax attention.

</details>


### [83] [Task-Specific Feature Fusion Method for Multi-Task Affective Behavior Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13986)
*Jiajun Sun,Zhe Gao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出任务自适应特征融合策略，利用冻结的预训练视觉骨干（DINOv2 ViT-L 和 DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base）为 ABAW11 挑战中的三个任务（效价-唤醒、分类表情、面部动作单元）分别选择最优的融合与预测方案，在验证集上取得了 1.6341 的总体分数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管情感相关的多任务（VA、EXPR、AU）在面部行为上自然关联，但实验表明它们需要不同的视觉特征、时序处理、融合机制和校准方法，因此需要为不同任务自适应选择架构而非强制共享。

Method: 1) 在外部表情数据集上微调 DINOv2 ViT-L 和 DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base，冻结后提取帧级特征；2) 系统比较多种帧级预测头、时序卷积头、后处理平滑、LightGBM、特征拼接/门控/残差融合、logit 融合、阈值校准及共享 MTL 结构；3) 为每个任务选择最优的融合与预测策略。

Result: 在 ABAW11 验证集上，EXPR macro-F1 0.4222，AU macro-F1 0.5402，mean VA CCC 0.6717，总体验证分数 1.6341。

Conclusion: 任务自适应融合冻结的视觉特征是一种简单而有效的 ABAW 风格多任务情感行为分析策略。

Abstract: The 11th Affective Behavior Analysis in-the-wild (ABAW11) Multi-Task Learning Challenge requires a unified system to predict valence-arousal, categorical expressions, and facial action units from the official s-Aff-Wild2 images. Although these tasks are naturally related through facial behavior, our validation experiments show that they benefit from different visual features, temporal processing strategies, fusion mechanisms, and calibration procedures. In this paper, we study task-adaptive feature fusion for ABAW11 multi-task affective behavior analysis. We first adapt two pretrained visual backbones, DINOv2 ViT-L and DINOv3 ConvNeXt-base, on an external expression-oriented facial image set and then freeze them to extract complementary frame-level features from the official ABAW11 data. On top of these frozen features, we systematically compare frame-level prediction heads, temporal convolutional heads, post-hoc temporal smoothing, LightGBM models, feature concatenation, gated fusion, residual fusion, late logit fusion, threshold calibration, and shared MTL structures. The final system selects task-specific fusion and prediction strategies rather than forcing all tasks to share a single architecture. On the ABAW11 validation set, the selected system achieves an EXPR macro-F1 of 0.4222, an AU macro-F1 of 0.5402, and a mean VA CCC of 0.6717, resulting in an overall validation score of 1.6341. The results suggest that task-adaptive fusion of frozen visual features is a simple and effective strategy for ABAW-style multi-task affective behavior analysis.

</details>


### [84] [From Pixels to States: Rethinking Interactive World Models as Game Engines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14076)
*Zhen Li,Zian Meng,Shuwei Shi,Mingliang Zhai,Jiaming Tan,Chuanhao Li,Kaipeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文以"动作-状态-观测"循环为组织视角，从玩家动作控制、游戏状态动态、状态-观测持久性和实时交互生成四个维度，系统分析了交互式游戏世界建模的研究现状，并提出了一个包含90小时《黑神话：悟空》游戏数据的可扩展数据引擎。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建能够对玩家动作做出连贯响应的交互式世界是计算机图形学、游戏和AI的共同目标。尽管视频生成模型被视为潜在的下代游戏引擎，但实现真正的交互式游戏世界需要遵循规则、具备长期一致性和实时性能，现有研究缺乏系统性的分析框架。

Method: 以"动作-状态-观测"循环为组织框架，从四个维度（玩家动作控制、游戏状态动态、状态-观测持久性、实时交互生成）分类分析现有方法，并构建了《黑神话：悟空》的大规模数据引擎（90+小时，含帧对齐的玩家动作、真实游戏状态、视觉观测及结构化和语义标注）。

Result: 系统梳理了交互式游戏世界建模的四个维度的现有方法家族及其优缺点，并提供了一个大规模、多模态、状态感知的游戏数据集作为研究资源。

Conclusion: 本文为交互式游戏世界建模提供了清晰的研究全景图，指出了现有方法的优势与权衡，并通过数据引擎的构建为状态感知的游戏世界建模提供了基础资源，有望推动该领域的发展。

Abstract: Building interactive worlds that respond coherently to player actions has long been a shared goal of computer graphics, games, and artificial intelligence. Recent video generative models provide a data-driven route toward this goal by predicting future observations conditioned on user actions, and are increasingly regarded as potential next-generation game engines. Realizing a genuinely interactive game world, however, requires interaction outcomes that follow rules over evolving game conditions, consequences that persist over long horizons, and a generation loop that operates in real time. Conventional game engines realize these properties through a recurrent action-state-observation loop, in which player actions update an explicit game state according to predefined rules and observations are rendered from the resulting state. Taking this loop as an organizing lens, this paper examines interactive game world modeling along four dimensions: player action control, game state dynamics, state-observation persistence, and real-time interactive generation. For each dimension, we start from the capabilities required by an interactive game world, group existing approaches into representative families, and discuss the strengths and trade-offs of each family. Complementing this analysis, we present a scalable data engine for Black Myth: Wukong that collects over 90 hours of gameplay with frame-aligned player actions, ground-truth game states, and visual observations, together with structured and semantic annotations, as a resource for state-aware game world modeling. We hope this paper offers a clear picture of where the field stands and fosters progress toward interactive game worlds.

</details>


### [85] [VideoRAE: Taming Video Foundation Models for Generative Modeling via Representation Autoencoders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.14088)
*Zhihao Xie,Junfeng Wu,Xinting Hu,Junchao Huang,Li Jiang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VideoRAE是一种利用冻结的视频基础模型（如V-JEPA 2）的多尺度层次特征，通过轻量级1D自注意力投影器将其压缩为紧凑、可重构且适合生成的视频潜变量的表示自编码器。它支持连续潜变量（用于扩散Transformer）和离散token（用于自回归模型），通过局部-全局表示对齐目标实现强重建性能，在UCF-101上取得了最优的类到视频gFVD，并比现有方法快约5倍收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统3D-VAE主要优化像素级重建，这限制了潜变量捕获的语义和时空结构。作者希望探索是否可以将冻结的视频基础模型（如V-JEPA 2、VideoMAEv2）的强理解能力转化为紧凑、可重构且适合生成的视频潜变量。

Method: 1) 使用冻结的视频基础编码器提取多尺度层次特征；2) 通过轻量级1D自注意力投影器压缩特征；3) 支持连续潜变量（用于DiT）和离散token（通过多码本高维量化用于AR模型）；4) 解码时使用局部-全局表示对齐目标（与冻结的VFM教师）来改善语义保留，无需KL正则化。

Result: 在UCF-101上，使用AR和DiT生成器分别获得40和93的最优类到视频gFVD，收敛速度比现有自编码器基线快约5倍。在2B规模文本到视频研究中，替换LTX-VAE为VideoRAE在相当设置下实现更快收敛。

Conclusion: 冻结的视频基础模型表示可以作为通用且适合生成的视频潜变量，验证了这一方向的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Video generative models commonly rely on latent spaces learned by 3D Variational Autoencoders (3D-VAEs). However, conventional 3D-VAEs are mainly optimized for pixel-level reconstruction, which can limit the semantic and spatio-temporal structure captured by their latents. Meanwhile, Video Foundation Models (VFMs) such as V-JEPA 2 and VideoMAEv2 show strong video understanding capabilities, yet whether their frozen representations can be transformed into compact, reconstruction-capable, and generation-friendly video latents remains largely unexplored. We answer this question with VideoRAE, a representation autoencoder that leverages multi-scale hierarchical features from a frozen video foundation encoder and compresses them with a lightweight 1D self-attention projector. VideoRAE supports both continuous latents for Diffusion Transformers and discrete tokens for autoregressive models via multi-codebook high-dimensional quantization. During decoding, a local-and-global representation alignment objective with the frozen VFM teacher improves semantic preservation and enables training without KL regularization. Experiments show that VideoRAE achieves strong reconstruction in both continuous and discrete regimes. On UCF-101, it obtains state-of-the-art class-to-video gFVDs of 40 and 93 with AR and DiT generators, respectively, while converging approximately 5x faster than competing autoencoder baselines. In a controlled 2B-scale text-to-video study, replacing LTX-VAE with VideoRAE leads to faster convergence under comparable settings. These results validate frozen VFM representations as versatile and generation-friendly video latents. The model and code will be released on https://zhxie0117.github.io/VideoRAE.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [86] [Autonomous UAV Route Planning for Coverage Maximization in Environmental Monitoring: A Systematic Literature Review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13054)
*Sebastian Jouannet-Contreras,Carola Figueroa-Flores*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项关于自主无人机覆盖路径规划的系统文献综述（SLR）的协议和初步结果，遵循PRISMA 2020框架，检索了2015-2026年间Scopus和Web of Science的相关研究。初步分析显示，现有研究主要集中在覆盖导向、多无人机协调和能量感知优化，而对天气、不确定性及复杂障碍环境的关注较少，且多依赖仿真验证，存在仿真到现实的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机环境监测需要能够最大化覆盖面积同时处理能量限制、操作约束和几何复杂性的路径规划方法，但该领域研究较为分散，缺乏系统性梳理，因此有必要进行结构化综述以识别成熟技术和未解决的空白。

Method: 采用PRISMA 2020框架进行系统文献综述，检索Scopus和Web of Science数据库，时间范围为2015-2026年，聚焦路径规划、覆盖路径规划和信息路径规划三个方向。目前已完成562条记录的去重（移除161条重复）、401条记录的标题/摘要/关键词筛选，保留247项研究进行全文评估。

Result: 初步分析表明：研究高度集中于覆盖导向公式、多无人机协调和能量感知优化；较少研究明确考虑天气、不确定性或障碍物丰富的环境；大多数保留研究依赖仿真验证，存在仿真-现实差距；近期研究对强化学习、混合优化和几何感知规划的兴趣增加。

Conclusion: 该领域研究活跃但碎片化，亟需结构化综合以识别适用于真实环境监测任务的成熟技术和未解决的关键缺口，特别是应对现实世界中天气变化、不确定性及复杂障碍环境的能力不足。

Abstract: Environmental monitoring with unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) requires route planning methods that maximize covered area while handling energy limits, operational constraints, and geometric complexity. This paper reports the protocol and preliminary results of an ongoing systematic literature review (SLR) on autonomous UAV route planning for coverage-oriented environmental monitoring. The review follows the PRISMA 2020 framework and searches Scopus and Web of Science for studies published between 2015 and 2026. The protocol focuses on path planning, coverage path planning, and informative path planning, with emphasis on algorithmic families, coverage and energy metrics, obstacle handling, geometric environment representations, and environmental constraints. At the current stage, 562 records have been identified, 161 duplicates have been removed, and 401 unique records have been screened by title, abstract, and keywords. From these, 247 studies were retained for full-text eligibility assessment (235 eligible and 12 borderline records to be resolved during full-text review). A preliminary analysis of the retained studies suggests strong concentration on coverage-oriented formulations, multi-UAV coordination, and energy-aware optimization, while fewer studies explicitly address weather, uncertainty, or obstacle-rich environments. Most retained studies rely on simulation-based validation, highlighting a potential simulation-to-reality gap, and recent publications show increasing interest in reinforcement learning, hybrid optimization, and geometry-aware planning. These early findings indicate an active but fragmented research landscape and support the need for a structured synthesis to identify mature techniques and unresolved gaps for realistic environmental monitoring missions.

</details>


### [87] [HRIBench: Benchmarking Interaction-Centric Human-Robot Collaboration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13056)
*Chang Liu,Jiawei Zhang,Tao Zhang,Ye Wang,Hongyu Zhou,Qin Jin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HRIBench是一个面向意图感知的人机协作诊断基准，通过结构化场景脚本建模交互角色、时间依赖和协调约束，包含13个角色条件任务和650+评估回合。实验表明现有基础策略在协作中表现显著不足，而微调可有效提升性能，真实世界应用也验证了其价值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA基准主要评估孤立操作技能，未建模人机交互结构，而真实协作需要共享代理下的意图理解、时间同步、协议遵循和安全交互，因此亟需一个专门面向人机协作意图感知的诊断基准。

Method: 将协作任务表示为结构化场景脚本，显式建模代理角色、时间依赖、协调约束和人类行为分布；定义Instructor、Collaborator、Intruder三种交互角色；引入同步性、响应性、协议遵从性和安全性等可解释交互中心指标；基于GR00T、pi0.5、ACT评估并支持微调。

Result: 包含13个角色条件任务，650+评估回合。当前基础策略在协作中表现显著不足，尤其在时间协调和意图感知行为方面。微调可改进协作性能。真实场景中，HRIBench模拟数据将GR00T N1.5成功率从0.10提升至0.43。

Conclusion: HRIBench有效揭示了现有策略在人机协作中的局限性，证明了面向交互的机器人学习基准的学术价值，并为未来研究提供了标准化评估框架。

Abstract: Current vision-language-action (VLA) benchmarks primarily evaluate isolated manipulation skills while leaving human-robot interaction structure largely unmodeled. However, real-world collaboration fundamentally requires coordination under shared agency, including intent understanding, temporal synchronization, protocol adherence, and safe interaction in dynamic environments. To address this gap, we introduce HRIBench, a diagnostic benchmark for intent-aware human-robot collaboration based on executable interaction scenarios. HRIBench represents collaborative tasks as structured scenario scripts that explicitly model agent roles, temporal dependencies, coordination constraints, and human behavior distributions. Building on this abstraction, HRIBench defines three representative interaction roles: Instructor, Collaborator, and Intruder, covering intent communication, joint coordination, and robustness under human intervention. The benchmark contains 13 role-conditioned tasks with over 650 evaluation episodes generated from diverse interaction trajectories and scene variations. Beyond binary task success, HRIBench introduces interpretable interaction-centric metrics spanning synchronization, responsiveness, protocol compliance, and safety. We evaluate adapted policies based on GR00T, pi0.5, and ACT under a unified protocol. Results show that current foundation robot policies struggle substantially in collaborative settings despite strong manipulation ability, revealing major limitations in temporal coordination and intent-aware behavior. Fine-tuning on HRIBench consistently improves collaborative performance. In a real-world adaptation study, simulation data generated by HRIBench improves GR00T N1.5's physical-task success rate from 0.10 to 0.43, demonstrating the benchmark's value for advancing interaction-centric robot learning.

</details>


### [88] [Power from Potential: A Survey of Electrostatic Actuators for Haptics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13058)
*Ahad M. Rauf,Ran Zhou,Eric Acome,Madeline Balaam,Sean Follmer,Teng Han,Craig Shultz,Daniel Leithinger*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文综述了高压静电致动器（HVEA）在触觉反馈领域的四类技术，分析其工作机制、性能特征及应用潜力，并提出未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统电机和温敏聚合物因体积大、功耗高，难以满足可穿戴触觉设备对柔软、轻薄、静音、节能的需求，而HVEA凭借快速、静音、低功耗及可定制化等优势成为有前景的替代方案。

Method: 对四类HVEA（静电可切换粘合剂、介电弹性体致动器、软电液致动器、电动力学泵）进行聚焦综述，从工作机制、带宽、力密度、空间可扩展性及触觉反馈适用性等方面进行跨技术对比分析。

Result: 识别出各类HVEA在人体工学、制造流程、自感知集成等方面的共同设计约束与新兴策略，明确其在触觉交互中的独特优势。

Conclusion: HVEA在推动触觉交互方面具有独特定位，但仍需进一步研究以将其转化为实用系统。

Abstract: As haptic interfaces integrate more seamlessly into wearables and everyday environments, they increasingly require actuators that are soft, thin, silent, and energy efficient. However, conventional motors and temperature-responsive polymers often struggle to deliver these properties due to their bulky form factors and high power consumption. High-Voltage Electrostatic Actuators (HVEAs), which generate force by applying an electric field to localized charge concentrations using high voltages and ultra-low currents, have recently emerged as a compelling alternative due to their fast, silent, and low-power operation within highly customizable and compliant form factors.
  This paper presents a focused review of HVEAs for haptics, examining four major classes: electrostatic switchable adhesives, dielectric elastomer actuators, soft electrohydraulic actuators, and electrokinetic pumps. For each class, we describe their mechanisms that enable haptic output; characterize their bandwidths, force densities, and spatial scalability; and evaluate their versatility for rendering cutaneous and kinesthetic feedback across wearable and world-grounded interfaces. Through this cross-technology analysis, we identify common design constraints and emerging strategies for improving ergonomics, streamlining fabrication, and integrating self-sensing. We conclude by outlining where HVEAs are uniquely positioned to advance haptic interaction and highlighting key research directions needed to translate these technologies into practical systems.

</details>


### [89] [GPUSimBench: Towards Scalable and Reliable GPU-Accelerated Simulators in Embodied AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13059)
*Huzhenyu Zhang,Shenghai Yuan,Wenrui Yan,Li Ma,Hengjie Li,Jingcheng Pang,Dmitry Yudin*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过引入 GPUSimBench 基准，揭示了主流 GPU 仿真器在扩展性、物理一致性和计算确定性方面的隐藏局限，并量化了 GPU 批处理执行带来的非确定性问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着数据驱动具身 AI 依靠 GPU 加速仿真器进行大规模并行训练，计算吞吐量提升的同时，并行效率、物理保真度和执行确定性之间的权衡尚未被充分研究，阻碍了可靠机器人学习的发展。

Method: 设计 GPUSimBench 基准，包含三个评估维度：1) 通过受控斜面任务建立物理基础评估，量化仿真动力学与真实世界的分布对齐；2) 测量跨环境数量的吞吐量和内存占用，评估并行可扩展性；3) 揭示并量化 GPU 批处理执行固有的非确定性，包括运行间和环境间的变异性。

Result: 发现 GPU 批处理执行存在显著的非确定性；识别出当前仿真器堆栈中四种随机性经验机制；表明无限制的扩展会在没有明确约束时损害可重复性。

Conclusion: GPU 仿真在追求大规模并行时需对非确定性问题施加明确约束，以保证机器人学习实验的可重复性与可靠性。

Abstract: Data-driven embodied AI is rapidly transitioning into a paradigm that scales training through massively parallel simulation, where GPU-accelerated simulators serve as the foundational data infrastructure. However, as computational throughput scales, the underlying trade-offs between parallel efficiency, physical fidelity, and execution determinism remain largely unexamined, hindering the development of reliable robot learning. In this paper, we expose the hidden limits of mainstream GPU-based robotic simulators (e.g., Isaac Lab, Genesis) by introducing GPUSimBench, which focuses on scalability, physical consistency, and computational determinism. First, GPUSimBench establishes a physical grounding evaluation with a controlled inclined-plane task, quantifying the distributional alignment between simulated dynamics and their real-world counterparts. Second, we benchmark parallel scalability by measuring throughput and memory footprints across scaling environment counts. Crucially, beyond standard performance metrics, we unveil and quantify the inherent non-determinism introduced by GPU-batched execution, characterized by significant run-to-run and inter-environment variability even under identical initial conditions. Finally, we identify four empirical regimes of stochasticity within current simulator stacks, highlighting that unbounded scaling can compromise reproducibility without explicit constraints.

</details>


### [90] [A Bayesian framework for the uncanny valley in humanoid robot design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13060)
*Shimon Honda,Rin Shibano,Hideyoshi Yanagisawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个层次贝叶斯生成模型，将恐怖谷理论的经验性指导原则转化为可操作的数学设计变量，通过模拟和人类实验验证了该模型能解释并调控恐怖谷效应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有恐怖谷设计准则（如避免过度逼真、减少跨模态不匹配）是经验性规则，缺乏可量化、可操作的数学表达，难以直接用于算法化机器人外观设计。

Method: 构建层次贝叶斯生成模型，将亲和力定义为后验加权的负类别条件惊奇度，将恐怖谷机制映射到四个变量：偏离预测机器人类别均值、跨模态人类相似度不一致、预测不确定性、观测不确定性。通过模拟和受试者实验（操控模糊先验刺激和模糊评估刺激）验证模型。

Result: 模拟显示类别模糊性和外观-运动不匹配会降低亲和力，不确定性会重塑恐怖谷曲线。人类实验表明：增加观测不确定性减弱了中等人类相似度时的熟悉度下降；低预测不确定性提高了类机器人外观的评分。

Conclusion: 该框架将恐怖谷的经验启发式转化为计算基础，可用于算法化评估和优化人形机器人的外观与行为。

Abstract: The uncanny valley is a long-standing empirical rule in humanoid robot design: making robots more human-like can reduce, rather than increase, affinity. Yet existing guidelines, such as adopting robot-like appearances, avoiding excessive realism, and reducing cross-modal mismatches, remain difficult to use for algorithmic design because they are not expressed as manipulable variables. Here, we propose a hierarchical Bayesian generative model that operationalizes these guidelines as mathematical design variables. The model represents affinity toward humanoid robots as posterior-weighted negative category-conditional surprise and explains category ambiguity and perceptual mismatch as increases in surprise. It maps uncanny-valley mechanisms onto four variables: deviation from the predicted robot-category mean, inconsistency in human likeness across modalities, prediction uncertainty, and observational uncertainty. Simulations showed that category ambiguity and appearance--motion mismatch can produce affinity reductions, and that uncertainty reshapes the valley. In a human-subject experiment with robot--human morphing images, we manipulated prediction uncertainty using blurred prior robot stimuli and observational uncertainty using blurred evaluation stimuli. Increased observational uncertainty attenuated the decrease in familiarity ratings at intermediate human likeness, whereas low prediction uncertainty increased ratings for robot-like appearances. This framework turns empirical uncanny-valley heuristics into a computational basis for algorithmically evaluating and optimizing humanoid robot appearance and behavior.

</details>


### [91] [A 3DGS-Driven Dynamic Viewpoint and Vibrotactile Framework for Subsea Teleoperation Validated via fNIRS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13067)
*Fang Xu,Tianyu Zhou,Ruitong Tian,Md Jahidul Islam,Jing Du*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种多模态远程操作架构，通过动态自适应视点系统（DAVS）和触觉反馈套件，显著提升水下机器人在高延迟环境下的操作性能和认知耐力。人类实验（N=30）表明，3DGS驱动的外心视点在严重延迟（0.5-1.0秒）下表现最优，而触觉反馈在低延迟时改善路径保持。fNIRS揭示延迟导致认知脱离效应，而DAVS能维持执行控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 水下机器人在洪水淹没、杂乱基础设施中远程操作受限于狭窄2D自我中心视角和海底通信延迟，现有方法缺乏对空间规划和边界规避的解耦，且高延迟下操作员认知负荷过载。

Method: 基于ROS-Unity框架，提出多模态遥操作架构：（1）动态自适应视点系统（DAVS），通过连续优化和实时3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）合成无遮挡的外心视角；（2）躯干振动触觉套件，将局部障碍物间隙映射为直观触觉提示。采用3×4重复测量设计（N=30），比较三种交互模态（自我中心、触觉、外心）在四种通信延迟下的效果。

Result: （1）触觉反馈在低延迟时改善路径保持；（2）3DGS驱动的外心可视化在严重延迟（0.5-1.0秒）下显著优于其他模态；（3）fNIRS显示传统自我中心操作在延迟增加时出现认知脱离（工作记忆过载、前额叶激活下降），而DAVS维持执行控制。

Conclusion: 空间扎根的多模态辅助能显著提升操作员在延迟退化水下遥操作中的性能和认知耐力，为恶劣环境下的远程操控提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Teleoperating remotely operated vehicles (ROVs) in flooded, cluttered infrastructure is fundamentally limited by narrow 2D egocentric views and subsea communication latency. We present a multimodal teleoperation architecture built on a ROS-Unity framework that decouples proactive spatial planning from reactive boundary avoidance. The system replaces static camera feeds with a Dynamic Adaptive Viewpoint System (DAVS), which uses continuous optimization and real-time 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) to synthesize an occlusion-free exocentric viewpoint from onboard state estimation. To further reduce sensory workload, a torso-mounted vibrotactile suit maps local obstacle clearance to intuitive haptic proximity cues. The architecture was evaluated in a controlled human-subject study (N = 30) using a BlueROV2 navigating a complex simulated underwater facility. A 3 x 4 repeated-measures design compared three interaction modalities (Egocentric, Haptic, Exocentric) under four communication delays (0.0-1.0 s). Performance was quantified using behavioral measures and functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) to assess task-evoked prefrontal activation. Results show that reactive haptic feedback improves path adherence under minimal delay, whereas the 3DGS-driven exocentric visualization provides superior resilience under severe latency (0.5-1.0 s), significantly outperforming the other modalities. fNIRS further revealed a cognitive disengagement effect: increasing latency during conventional egocentric teleoperation overloaded working memory and reduced prefrontal activation, whereas the proactive spatial context provided by DAVS sustained executive control. These findings demonstrate that spatially grounded, multimodal assistance can substantially improve operator performance and cognitive endurance during latency-degraded underwater teleoperation.

</details>


### [92] [HRO: Hierarchical Room-to-Object Framework for Zero-Shot Object Goal Navigation with Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13072)
*Luyuan Jia,Yinfeng Yu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型的层次化房间到物体（HRO）框架，用于零样本目标导航，通过粗到细的方式引导智能体先定位房间再寻找目标物体，在Gibson和HM3D数据集上取得了优于现有方法的导航成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于LLM的零样本目标导航方法仅将LLM作为平面推理工具，缺乏类似人类的空间语义层次认知（从房间到物体），导致探索盲目性强、语义关联准确性不足，未能充分发挥LLM的常识推理潜力。

Method: 提出HRO框架，利用LLM驱动层次化的"房间→物体"推理策略，引导智能体按照从粗粒度（房间语义）到细粒度（具体物体）的方式进行探索和导航。

Result: 在Gibson和HM3D数据集上验证，HRO框架在成功率和泛化性方面均优于现有基于LLM的方法。

Conclusion: 层次化的空间认知建模能有效释放LLM在零样本目标导航任务中的常识推理潜力，实现更高效、更准确的目标定位。

Abstract: Zero-shot object-goal navigation aims to enable an intelligent agent to explore and navigate to objects of unknown categories in an unfamiliar environment without specific target training. In zero-shot navigation tasks, pre-trained large models are usually employed to leverage their prior knowledge for guiding the agent's navigation. However, existing zero-shot object-goal navigation methods based on large language models (LLMs) merely utilize LLMs as flat reasoning tools to directly associate objects or regions. They lack the hierarchical spatial cognition modeling of human-like room semantics to object localization, which leads to strong blindness in exploration, insufficient accuracy in semantic association, and failure to fully unleash the common-sense reasoning potential of LLMs. This paper proposes an LLM-driven hierarchical room-to-object (HRO) framework for zero-shot object-goal navigation, which guides the agent to explore and navigate to the target object in a coarse-to-fine manner. Experiments on Gibson and HM3D datasets verify that our HRO framework achieves superior success rate and generalization over existing LLM-based methods, underscoring LLMs' strong potential for zero-shot object-goal navigation.

</details>


### [93] [Worlds in One Demo: A Synthetic Data Engine for Learning Open-World Mobile Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13154)
*Lingxiao Guo,Huanyu Li,Guanya Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WANDA是一种从单次真实演示中生成合成数据的方法，通过重建背景高斯溅射和机器人-物体交互轨迹，再利用全身运动规划和纠正性状态扩展生成多样化数据，从而训练出具有长程鲁棒性、空间泛化和跨环境泛化能力的移动操作策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥操作和UMI数据采集范式需要大量人力成本，难以规模化扩展。本文旨在最大化每次人工演示的价值，通过合成数据引擎突破人工数据采集的限制。

Method: 1) 从RGBD观测重建背景高斯溅射和交互轨迹作为世界基板；2) 利用全身运动规划将接触丰富的交互片段重排为新的空间配置轨迹；3) 采用纠正性状态扩展增加机器人和物体状态多样性以增强长程鲁棒性；4) 在生成的多样化3D世界上合成轨迹实现跨环境泛化；5) 通过合成渲染与高斯溅射背景合成逼真观察。

Result: 在多种仿真和真实世界任务中，WANDA训练的策略实现了长程鲁棒性、广泛的空间泛化和跨环境泛化；并支持跨本体零样本部署。

Conclusion: WANDA通过合成数据引擎大幅提升单次演示的数据价值，为移动操作策略学习提供了一种可扩展、低成本的数据生成范式。

Abstract: Learning open-world mobile manipulation policies requires vast data to achieve spatial generalization, long-horizon robustness, and scene generalization. Current prevailing data collection paradigms, teleoperation and UMI, demand prohibitive human effort and cost at scale. To scale beyond the limits of manual data collection, we seek to maximize the value of each human demonstration by scalable data generation. To this end, we introduce WANDA: learning open-World mobile mANipulation from one demonstration via a synthetic DAta engine. WANDA first reconstructs background Gaussian splats and robot-object interaction trajectories from source RGBD observations, as a world substrate for later planning and rendering. It then rearranges contact-rich robot-object interaction segments into extensive spatial configurations, utilizing whole-body motion planning to chain them into new trajectories. To enhance long-horizon robustness, it applies Corrective State Expansion to increase the robot and object state diversity at different stages of mobile manipulation. To unlock cross-environment generalization, trajectories are synthesized on diverse generated 3D worlds from everyday photos. Furthermore, we synthesize photo-realistic observations by compositing rendered robot and object meshes with Gaussian splatting backgrounds. We evaluate our approach on extensive simulation and real-world tasks in various scenes. Experiments show that policies trained with WANDA achieve long-horizon robustness, broad spatial generalization and cross-environment generalization from one real demonstration. Moreover, WANDA naturally supports cross-embodiment data generation, validated by zero-shot deployment on another mobile manipulator with a distinct morphology.

</details>


### [94] [Attitude Estimation Using Inertial and Barometric Measurements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13254)
*Melone Nyoba Tchonkeu,Soulaimane Berkane,Tarek Hamel*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出两种基于气压计辅助的姿态估计算法（确定性Riccati观测器+互补滤波级联结构，以及SO(3)×R²上的非线性观测器），在GNSS失效和强加速条件下利用气压高度测量提升姿态估计精度与稳定性，并通过仿真与实飞数据验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主飞行器在GNSS拒止及强加速条件下，仅靠IMU无法区分重力与惯性加速度导致姿态估计不准确；现有辅助速度传感器（GNSS、皮托管等）存在不可用、间歇性或成本高的问题，亟需一种轻量、可靠的替代方案。

Method: 1) 设计确定性Riccati观测器与互补滤波器级联结构，在均匀可观性条件下实现几乎全局渐近稳定（AGAS）；2) 提出SO(3)×R²上的非线性观测器，将IMU作为输入、气压计与磁力计作为输出，在宽松均匀可观性条件下保证局部指数稳定（LES）。

Result: 仿真与实飞数据验证表明，气压计辅助的姿态估计在最小传感器配置下提供轻量、可靠且有效的补充感知模态，在传统速度测量不可用或退化时展现出实用替代价值。

Conclusion: 气压计辅助的姿态估计架构有效解决了GNSS拒止/强加速条件下的姿态估计难题，为自主飞行器提供了一种低成本、轻量级且鲁棒的替代方案。

Abstract: Accurate and robust attitude estimation is a key challenge for autonomous vehicles, particularly in GNSS-denied conditions and during highly accelerated flight. In such conditions, Inertial Measurement Units (IMUs) alone are insufficient for reliable tilt estimation due to the ambiguity between gravitational and inertial accelerations. Although auxiliary velocity sensors such as GNSS, Pitot tubes, Doppler radar, or Visual Inertial Odometry are commonly used, they may be unavailable, intermittent, or costly. This paper introduces a barometer-aided attitude estimation architecture that exploits barometric altitude measurements to provide complementary information on the vehicle's vertical motion, thereby enhancing attitude estimation within nonlinear observers on SO(3). The contributions are twofold. First, we design a deterministic Riccati observer cascaded with a complementary filter, ensuring almost-global asymptotic stability (AGAS) under a uniform observability (UO) condition while preserving the geometric structure of the attitude dynamics. Second, we propose a nonlinear observer evolving on SO(3)xR2, which integrates IMU measurements as inputs and barometer and magnetometer measurements as outputs within a unified framework, guaranteeing local exponential stability (LES) under relaxed uniform observability conditions. The proposed approaches are validated using both simulated and real flight data. The results demonstrate that barometer-aided estimation provides a lightweight, reliable, and effective complementary sensing modality for attitude estimation in minimal-sensing configurations, offering a practical alternative when conventional velocity measurements are unavailable or degraded.

</details>


### [95] [Parsimonious disturbance-aware minimum-time planning with parametric uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13312)
*Martino Gulisano,Matteo Masoni,Marco Gabiccini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出并验证了一种用于赛车的鲁棒最小圈时规划框架，通过考虑状态扰动和参数不确定性（转动惯量、质心位置、空气阻力系数），并采用空间选择性激活策略降低计算复杂度。实验表明，相比无鲁棒性的参考轨迹，该框架在保证圈时代价可控的前提下显著提升了赛道跟踪的可靠性与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的最小圈时规划方法未充分考虑车辆参数不确定性（如转动惯量、质心位置、空气阻力系数）对赛道跟踪性能的影响，导致在实际场景中轨迹可跟踪性差、失败率高。

Method: 在已有的扰动感知框架基础上，将参数不确定性纳入随机车辆动力学传播，并在每个轨迹点收紧轮胎摩擦约束（基于最坏情况）。为保持计算可行性，采用空间选择性、节俭的激活策略，仅在关键路段施加鲁棒约束。

Result: 使用MPC作为虚拟测试驾驶员，在巴塞罗那-加泰罗尼亚赛道的典型区段进行了1000次随机脉冲扰动和参数散布的仿真。鲁棒规划相比无鲁棒规划，失败运行次数显著减少，车辆输入和轴饱和度的信号分散度更低，可跟踪性更好，仅付出适度的圈时成本。

Conclusion: 所提出的鲁棒最小圈时规划框架在保证计算效率的同时，有效提升了参考轨迹在参数和状态不确定性下的可跟踪性与鲁棒性，适用于实际赛车应用场景。

Abstract: This study presents and validates a minimum-lap-time planning (MLTP) framework for motorsport applications that embeds robustness against both state disturbances and parameter uncertainty. The methodology builds upon a prior disturbance-aware framework that, at each track point, propagates stochastic vehicle dynamics over a short horizon and tightens tyre-friction constraints based on the worst-case scenario at horizon end. We extend the formulation to account for uncertainty in key vehicle parameters: moment of inertia, centre-of-mass position, and aerodynamic drag coefficient. To keep the extended formulation computationally tractable, a spatially selective, parsimonious activation strategy confines the robust constraints to the circuit segments where they are most critical. We demonstrate the improved driveability of the robust references by employing a model predictive controller (MPC) as a virtual test driver. For each reference, the same MPC drives a simulated FSAE (Formula SAE) car over 1000 runs on a representative Barcelona-Catalunya sector, with randomly realised impulsive disturbances and parameter scatter. We compare a nominal reference, planned without robustness, against its robust counterparts. The latter yield consistently fewer failed runs and, at a moderate sector-time cost, show tighter dispersion of key signals (vehicle inputs, axle saturations) around the reference values, evidence of better trackability.

</details>


### [96] [Adapting Generalist Vehicle Models for High-Speed MPC Across Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13319)
*Rwik Rana,Jesse Quattrociocchi,Christian Ellis,Nathan Tsoi,Garrett Warnell,Joydeep Biswas*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: OptCar 提出了一种从通用运动学模型微调为专用模型的方案，通过历史条件动态适配模块和少量真实数据+合成数据，在越野高速场景下显著降低轨迹跟踪误差，并保持跨地形的泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的前向运动学预测基础模型（如AnyCar）虽能从通用模型专用化到目标平台，但有效专用化困难：需要大量真实数据，且适配后的模型易过拟合到特定地形或驾驶模式，难以兼顾跨地形泛化与专用性能。

Method: 提出OptCar方法：1) 引入历史条件动态适配模块（history-conditioned dynamics adaptation module），将近期状态-动作观测编码为动态上下文token；2) 使用少量真实数据结合环境特定系统辨识生成的合成轨迹，对通用模型进行微调。

Result: 在三种地形及带车外货物的分布外任务中，6 m/s高速下表现最佳。在植被和泥土等滑移多变地形上，轨迹跟踪误差相比AnyCar基线降低约55%；仅需每地形5分钟真实数据，在道路上即可媲美30分钟数据训练的专用模型，且地形变化时大幅超越后者。

Conclusion: OptCar有效弥合了通用与专用前向运动学模型之间的差距，在保持跨地形泛化能力的同时提升了特定车辆的动态预测与控制性能，尤其在高滑移高速场景下优势显著。

Abstract: High-speed off-road autonomy requires precise closed-loop control for a target vehicle while remaining robust across changing terrains. Recent forward kinodynamic (FKD) prediction foundation models suggest a promising path, starting from a generalist model and specializing it to the target platform. However, effective specialization remains challenging, as it often requires substantial real-world data, and models adapted to one setting can still overfit to specific terrains or driving regimes. We present OptCar (Optimized Car), a recipe for bridging the gap from generalist to specialist FKD models that preserves cross-terrain generalization while optimizing performance for a specific vehicle. $\texttt{OptCar}$ introduces a history-conditioned dynamics adaptation module that encodes recent state-action observations into a dynamics context token, and then fine-tunes the generalist model using limited real-world data together with targeted synthetic rollouts from environment-specific system identification. In closed-loop model predictive control (MPC) experiments across three terrains and an out-of-distribution cart-pulling task, the largest gains appear at 6~m/s, the highest speed evaluated and the regime in which slip dominates tracking error. On vegetation and dirt, the most slip-diverse terrain, OptCar reduces 6~m/s trajectory tracking error by roughly 55% relative to a fine-tuned AnyCar baseline, and remains the most accurate even when an unseen cart payload changes the dynamics. With only 5 minutes of real data per terrain, OptCar is competitive on road with a specialist trained on 30 minutes of road data, and substantially outperforms it once the terrain changes.

</details>


### [97] [Design and Characterization of a Limb Encircling Actuator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13337)
*Japmanjeet Singh Gill,Gray Cortright Thomas,Nikko Van Crey,Roberto Leo Medrano,Elliott J. Rouse*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文探索了一种新型的外骨骼驱动布局——肢体环绕驱动器（LEA），将执行器环绕肢体正交放置，以减少设备空间占用。虽然实现了7.5 Nm/kg的高连续力矩密度，但仍难以紧贴身体，揭示了此类设计在靠近人体方面的机遇与挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统下肢外骨骼的驱动组件通常位于腿部外侧或腰部/背部，超出身体自然轮廓，导致设备在日常使用中显得笨重，可能限制社会接受度。因此，需要重新思考驱动几何布局，使外骨骼更贴合身体。

Method: 提出肢体环绕驱动器（LEA），将执行器以与肢体轴线正交的平面环绕肢体，减小空间占用；采用新型径向轴承布局，可作为传统大直径轴承的轻量/低成本替代方案；通过自制测试台进行机电特性表征。

Result: LEA达到7.5 Nm/kg的连续力矩密度，质量为894 g。尽管实现了高力矩密度和创新布局，系统仍难以紧密贴合身体。

Conclusion: 肢体环绕驱动在靠近人体方面存在挑战，若克服驱动器尺寸和几何问题，有望实现更易融入日常服饰的高力矩密度外骨骼设计。

Abstract: Lower-limb powered exoskeletons have demonstrated substantial improvements in mobility and function, but most designs place actuation components lateral to the legs or remotely at the waist or back. These configurations often extend beyond the body's natural envelope, making devices intrusive in everyday use and potentially limiting societal adoption. We posit that rethinking actuation geometry could enable exoskeletons that conform more closely to the body. Here, we explored an actuation layout in which the actuator encircles the limb in a plane orthogonal to the limb axis, potentially reducing its spatial footprint around the body. We developed the Limb-Encircling Actuator (LEA) and characterized its electromechanical properties using a custom-built testbed. The LEA also features a novel radial bearing layout with potential as a lightweight or lower-cost alternative to traditional large-diameter bearings. The actuator achieved a continuous torque density of 7.5 Nm/kg with a mass of 894 g. Despite this high torque density and innovative layout, the system remained difficult to contain close to the body. These results highlight opportunities and challenges in limb-encircling actuation and provide insights into torque-dense exoskeleton designs that could integrate more readily into everyday apparel if challenges in actuator sizing and geometry are overcome.

</details>


### [98] [Safe Overtaking for Autonomous Racing Using Hierarchical Optimization and Learning-Based Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13348)
*Hassan Jardali,Kai Yin,Lantao Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出分层式自动驾驶赛车超车框架，上层MIQP解决超车路径选择，下层非线性MPC结合自适应离散时间CBF（通过强化学习在线调整衰减参数），在保证安全的前提下降低保守性，提升超车性能，实验证明自适应策略在不同赛道上的综合成功率最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MPC+CBF方法中固定衰减的离散时间CBF在交互式赛车场景中过于保守，限制了超车性能，且需要针对不同赛道手动调参。

Method: 提出分层框架：1）上层MIQP选择可行超车拓扑（组合优化）；2）下层Frenet坐标系下的非线性MPC嵌入离散时间CBF约束；3）强化学习策略在线自适应调整CBF衰减参数，避免直接控制车辆输入。

Result: 仿真与硬件实验表明，单一固定衰减参数无法在所有赛道上取得均衡表现，而自适应策略实现了最高综合成功率，并维持了稳健的安全-性能权衡，无需每赛道调参。

Conclusion: 分层分解（组合决策+连续轨迹优化）结合CBF衰减参数的自适应学习，能有效降低保守性、提升鲁棒性，在保证名义安全约束的前提下适应环境变化。

Abstract: Autonomous racing overtaking requires balancing competitive performance with safety under nonlinear vehicle dynamics and real-time constraints. Model Predictive Control (MPC) combined with Control Barrier Functions (CBFs) provides a principled mechanism for certifying forward invariance of a safe set. However, commonly used fixed-decay discrete-time CBF formulations can become overly conservative in interactive racing scenarios, limiting overtaking performance and requiring manual tuning across track conditions. This paper proposes a hierarchical overtaking framework that explicitly separates maneuver-level decision making from safety-certified trajectory control, reducing conservatism while preserving safety. A high-level Mixed-Integer Quadratic Program (MIQP) resolves the combinatorial passing-side selection problem by selecting a feasible overtaking topology, while a nonlinear Frenet-frame MPC enforces vehicle dynamics and safety through embedded discrete-time CBF constraints. This decomposition isolates the combinatorial complexity of maneuver selection from the continuous trajectory optimization. To further mitigate the sensitivity of fixed-decay barrier constraints, a reinforcement learning policy adapts the discrete-time CBF decay parameter online, enabling context-dependent modulation of safety margins without directly controlling vehicle inputs. Simulation and scaled-hardware experiments show that no single fixed decay parameter achieves uniformly strong performance across tracks, whereas the adaptive strategy attains the highest aggregate success rate and consistently strong safety--performance trade-offs without per-track tuning, improving robustness to environment variation while maintaining safety constraint satisfaction in nominal operation.

</details>


### [99] [A Hybrid Sampling-Based Trajectory Planner with Game-Theoretic Guidance for Autonomous Racing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13354)
*Alexander Langmann,Frederico Pita de Araujo,Mattia Piccinini,Johannes Betz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种混合架构，将博弈论推理集成到基于采样的运动规划器中，通过离线学习势函数和在线梯度优化生成交互参考路径，在不增加计算负担的情况下实现自动驾驶赛车的防御性行为（如阻挡）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶赛车需要在极限操控下平衡车辆动力学与多智能体竞争中的策略决策，但直接在线求解完整动态博弈在计算上不可行，难以集成到高频自主软件栈中。

Method: 基于α-势能博弈公式，离线学习交互势函数，在线通过梯度优化动态调整交互参数生成"交互参考路径"，并将其作为动态成本偏差集成到高频采样规划器中。

Result: 在Yas Marina赛道的高保真仿真环境中验证，定性和定量结果表明该方法成功实现了阻挡等防御性行为，且计算负担远低于完整动态博弈求解器。

Conclusion: 所提混合架构在不牺牲计算效率的前提下，有效实现了博弈论级别的策略交互行为，为自动驾驶赛车规划提供了一种实用且可扩展的解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous racing demands planning algorithms that balance vehicle dynamics at the limits of handling with strategic decision-making in competitive multi-agent scenarios. Game theory provides a mathematical framework for modeling these interactions, enabling interactive trajectory planning and strategic behaviors, such as blocking. However, directly solving full dynamic games online is computationally prohibitive and challenging to integrate into robust, high-frequency autonomous software stacks. This paper proposes a hybrid architecture that integrates game-theoretic reasoning into a sampling-based motion planner, combining strategic interactions with robust trajectory generation. Building upon an $α$-potential game formulation, we utilize an offline-learned potential function to capture multi-agent interactions. During online operation, a gradient-based optimization dynamically refines interaction parameters to generate an \textit{Interaction Reference Path}. This path serves as a dynamic cost bias within a high-frequency sampling planner. We evaluate our approach in a high-fidelity simulation environment on the Yas Marina Circuit. Qualitative and quantitative results demonstrate that our approach successfully induces defensive behaviors like blocking without carrying the computational burden of full dynamic game solvers.

</details>


### [100] [Min-Max Regret Task Allocation and Planning of Heterogeneous Multi-Robot System in Partially Known Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13403)
*Xinkai Liang,Huixuan Chan,Ying Liu,Yangxi Shi,Hao Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向大规模异构多机器人系统（HMRS）的鲁棒规划框架，通过最小最大遗憾优化、区域绑定原子命题（RbAP）以及基于遗憾的分支定界策略（E-PDT），在部分已知环境中高效处理复杂时序逻辑任务，实现了近乎线性的可扩展性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有方法在处理部分已知环境下的复杂时序逻辑任务时，难以平衡探索不确定区域与利用已知资源之间的矛盾，且面临指数级计算复杂度的瓶颈。

Method: 将问题建模为最小最大遗憾优化，提出区域绑定原子命题（RbAP）将资源不确定性融入自动机结构；设计扩展规划决策树（E-PDT）并搭配基于遗憾的分支定界（BnB）策略，动态剪枝次优策略以平衡探索与利用。

Result: 理论分析证明了方法的可行性与完备性；数值与物理实验表明，该方法在机器人数量和类型上实现近乎线性的可扩展性，在解质量和计算效率上显著优于基于MILP的基线方法。

Conclusion: 该框架有效解决了大规模HMRS在部分已知环境下的时序任务分配难题，兼顾了鲁棒性与可扩展性。

Abstract: Efficient task allocation for large-scale Heterogeneous Multi-Robot Systems (HMRS) is critical, yet dealing with complex temporal logic tasks in partially known environment (PKE) remains a computational bottleneck. Existing approaches often struggle to balance exploring uncertain regions and exploiting known resources, while also suffering from exponential computational complexity. To address these issues, this paper presents a robust planning framework that simultaneously handles high-level logical constraints and environmental uncertainty without sacrificing scalability. We formulate the problem as a min-max regret optimization, proposing a Region-Binding Atomic Proposition (RbAP) to capture resource uncertainty within the automaton structure. To solve this, we propose the Extended Planning Decision Tree (E-PDT) equipped with a novel Regret-based Branch-and-Bound (BnB) strategy. Unlike traditional methods that rely on prior probabilities or worst-case analysis, our approach dynamically prunes suboptimal policies, effectively balancing the need for information gathering (exploration) and task completion (exploitation). Theoretical analysis confirms the feasibility and completeness of our approach. Extensive numerical and physical experiments demonstrate that the proposed framework achieves near-linear scalability with respect to the number of robots and types, significantly outperforming MILP-based baselines in both solution quality and computational efficiency.

</details>


### [101] [WNOJ-LIO: A White-Noise-on-Jerk Motion-Prior EKF for High-Dynamic LiDAR-IMU Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13405)
*Junning Lyu,Qizhi Guo,Xia Ning,Tao Song,Shaoming He*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WNOJ-LIO，一种基于白噪声加加速度（WNOJ）扩展卡尔曼滤波的LiDAR-IMU融合框架。与传统方法将IMU用于状态传播不同，WNOJ-LIO将IMU视为高频测量源，采用解耦的WNOJ先验进行状态预测，实现了闭环协方差传播，有效解决了高动态驾驶下的扫描失真和惯性噪声问题。在仿真和最高时速208km/h的真实赛车实验中验证了其优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高动态驾驶场景中，传统LIO方法存在两个耦合挑战：帧内运动扫描失真和受振动污染的惯性测量。现有实时LiDAR-IMU管线通过积分原始IMU测量来传播系统状态并用传播轨迹进行点云去畸变，这会将惯性噪声传播到校正后的扫描及后续的扫描到地图配准中，造成误差累积。

Method: 提出WNOJ-LIO框架，核心创新在于：(1) 使用解耦的WNOJ（白噪声加加速度）先验在R³×SO(3)流形上进行状态预测；(2) 将IMU作为高频测量源而非状态传播驱动；(3) 解耦的过程模型支持闭式协方差传播，弥合了批量WNOJ高斯过程轨迹先验与递归滤波之间的差距。

Result: 仿真结果表明在加速度/角速度去噪、扫描去畸变和定位精度上均优于FAST-LIO风格基线。真实世界实验在最高时速53~208km/h的四个赛车路段上进行，覆盖了广泛的车辆振动水平，进一步验证了该方法在高动态驾驶下对加速度、角速度、体线速度、姿态和位置的估计性能。

Conclusion: WNOJ-LIO通过重新设计LiDAR-IMU融合架构，有效解决了高动态场景下的噪声耦合问题，在极端驾驶条件下实现了更优的状态估计精度。代码已开源。

Abstract: LiDAR-inertial odometry (LIO) is a key component of autonomous navigation, but high-dynamic driving exposes two coupled challenges: intra-scan motion distortion and vibration-contaminated inertial measurements. Most real-time LiDAR-inertial pipelines propagate the system state by integrating raw IMU measurements and then use the propagated trajectory for point cloud de-distortion, thereby propagating inertial noise into both the corrected scan and the subsequent scan-to-map registration. This paper presents WNOJ-LIO, a LiDAR-IMU fusion framework based on a White-Noise-on-Jerk (WNOJ) Extended Kalman Filter (EKF). WNOJ-LIO employs a decoupled WNOJ prior on $\R^3 \times \SO(3)$ for state prediction and treats the IMU as a high-frequency measurement source rather than the driver of state propagation. The resulting posterior state history is then used for LiDAR scan de-distortion and subsequent point-to-plane LiDAR updates. The decoupled process model enables closed-form covariance propagation, thereby bridging the gap between batch WNOJ Gaussian process (GP) trajectory priors and recursive filtering.
  Simulation results demonstrate improvements in acceleration and angular-velocity denoising, scan de-distortion, and localization accuracy over a FAST-LIO-style baseline.
  Real-world experiments were conducted using an autonomous racing car on four driving segments with maximum speeds ranging from 53 to 208~km/h, covering a wide range of vehicle vibration levels. The experiments further validate the proposed method and provide a comprehensive evaluation of its performance in estimating acceleration, angular velocity, body-frame linear velocity, attitude, and position under highly dynamic driving. The source code of WNOJ-LIO is publicly available at https://github.com/LvJohny/wnoj-ekf-lio.git.

</details>


### [102] [Ego-Dynamics-Augmented World Model for Autonomous Driving with Zero-Shot Cross-Chassis Adaptation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13410)
*Zhidong Wang,Jingsong Liang,Zirui Li,Zhan Chen,Han Yu,Chen Lv*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: DynaDreamer通过显式的自车动力学先验增强Dreamer风格的世界模型，将自车运动与场景动态解耦，提升了想象精度和驾驶成功率，并实现了零样本跨底盘迁移。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有驾驶世界模型基于BEV表示，其帧间变换耦合了自车运动与场景动态，导致模型需要用大量容量恢复自车运动，损失了场景建模保真度和想象精度。

Method: 提出DynaDreamer方法，在Dreamer风格世界模型中引入物理启发式的自车动力学编码器-解码器，提取紧凑可识别的自车状态上下文，因果Transformer世界模型将此上下文作为先验和后验隐变量的条件。想象时通过动力学预测器传播上下文，使自车动力学先验与展开同步。

Result: 在城区和高速驾驶场景中，任务成功率分别比最强基线提升28%和61%；在未见底盘泛化场景中优势高达73%。

Conclusion: 显式解耦自车动力学可有效降低世界模型的预测熵和先验-后验KL散度，将建模负担集中于场景动态，同时实现零样本跨底盘自适应。

Abstract: World model (WM)-based reinforcement learning enables sample-efficient end-to-end autonomous driving learning by imagining long-horizon trajectories in latent space. However, most driving WMs operate on bird's-eye-view (BEV) representations that are inherently egocentric: the transition between consecutive frames entangles the ego vehicle's own motion with scene dynamics. As a result, the WM devotes significant capacity to recovering ego-motion from warped observations, at the cost of scene modeling fidelity and imagination accuracy. This work proposes DynaDreamer, a dynamics-augmented Dreamer-style reinforcement learning method to address this problem by augmenting the WM with an explicit ego-dynamics prior. A physics-informed ego-dynamics encoder-decoder extracts the ego-state history into a compact and identifiable context, which modulates a causal Transformer WM to condition both its prior and posterior latents. During imagination, the ego-dynamics predictor propagates this context forward to keep the ego-dynamics prior synchronized with the rollout. An information-theoretic analysis shows that conditioning on this context reduces both the predictive entropy of the observation transition and the prior--posterior Kullback--Leibler divergence, confining the WM's modeling burden to the scene dynamics beyond ego-motion. An additional benefit is zero-shot cross-chassis adaptation: the ego-dynamics context depends on identifiable chassis parameters, so that a vehicle with previously unseen dynamic characteristics can adapt the WM to the new chassis without retraining. Experiments demonstrate that DynaDreamer improves task success rates over the strongest baseline by 28% and 61% in urban and highway driving scenarios, respectively, with the advantage rising to 73% when extrapolating to unseen chassis.

</details>


### [103] [Generalizable VLA Finetuning via Representation Anchoring and Language-Action Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13429)
*Dwip Dalal,Shivansh Patel,Chahit Jain,Jeonghwan Kim,Utkarsh Mishra,Alex Baratian,Hyeonjeong Ha,Heng Ji,Svetlana Lazebnik,Unnat Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 针对VLM微调为VLA策略时出现的表征漂移和语言-动作不对齐问题，提出Anchor-Align方法，通过冻结VLM的知识蒸馏和语言-动作对齐训练，在物理机器人和大规模仿真中显著提升了任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA策略在BC微调过程中逐渐覆盖了预训练的表征，导致视觉和语义泛化能力下降；而简单的网络图文联合训练无法解决语言与动作之间的不对齐问题，现有基准测试也无法暴露这一缺陷。

Method: 提出Anchor-Align，包含两个目标：(1) Vision-Language Anchoring：从冻结的VLM副本逐层蒸馏表征，防止预训练表征漂移；(2) Language-Action Alignment：将每个动作目标转化为离散的运动方向标签，在同一机器人观测上联合训练语言和动作预测。

Result: 在物理xArm7机器人上，两种VLA架构的成功率分别从28%提升至54%、37%提升至60%；在LIBERO-PRO、LIBERO-Plus和CALVIN仿真基准上，在OOD扰动、感知鲁棒性和长程控制方面均有一致提升。

Conclusion: 保留预训练表征与有效的动作学习并非根本对立，通过合理的对齐策略可以兼顾两者。

Abstract: Finetuning a pretrained vision-language model (VLM) on robot demonstrations via behavior cloning (BC) has become the standard recipe for vision-language-action (VLA) policies. However, BC finetuning progressively overwrites the pretrained representations that support visual and semantic generalization. Co-training on web image-text data, a common remedy, does not prevent this; it applies language and action losses to separate observations, leaving VLAs with language-action misalignment that standard manipulation benchmarks do not expose. We propose Anchor-Align, which augments BC with two objectives: Vision-Language Anchoring distills layer-wise representations from a frozen VLM copy to prevent this drift, while Language-Action Alignment converts each action target into a discrete motion-direction label and jointly trains language and action prediction on the same robot observation. On a physical xArm7 robot, across two widely used VLA architectures, Anchor-Align improves real-robot success on both (28% to 54% and 37% to 60%). At scale in simulation, we demonstrate consistent improvements on OOD perturbations, perceptual robustness, and long-horizon control across LIBERO-PRO, LIBERO-Plus, and CALVIN, respectively, suggesting that preserving pretrained representations and effective action learning are not fundamentally at odds. Project page: anchoralignvla.github.io

</details>


### [104] [Stress-Sharing: A Bio-Inspired Approach to Decentralized Fault Repair in Modular Spacecraft](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.13444)
*Sidhdharth D. Sikka,Yue Shen,Shaoshuai Mou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种受生物伤口愈合启发的分布式应力共享修复策略，使模块化航天器在局部信息下自主修复结构损伤，仿真表明即使30%模块随机故障仍可聚集约80%幸存模块。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有修复方法依赖冗余或预规划，无法在局部信息和物理约束下实现自主修复，亟需一种去中心化的自修复策略。

Method: 将航天器建模为晶格约束图，提出去中心化异步应力共享修复策略：局部 distress 信号引导幸存模块向受损区域迁移以闭合间隙，之后各模块局部回溯自身运动以恢复损伤前形状，仅利用局部信息且无需绝对位置传感。

Result: 在PyBullet仿真中测试了多达160模块的结构、三种故障密度(10%,20%,30%)及随机/局部损伤。最严重情况下(30%随机故障)仍能聚集约80%以上幸存模块为单一连通体，且比例随规模增大而提升。

Conclusion: 该方法作为大规模模块化航天器的群体级修复策略具有良好适应性，能有效应对高比例随机故障。

Abstract: Structural damage in modular spacecraft can disrupt mechanical and communication connectivity, reducing system capability. Existing approaches rely on redundancy or preplanned reconfiguration and do not enable autonomous repair under local information and physical constraints. We model the spacecraft as a lattice-constrained graph and introduce a fully decentralized, asynchronous stress-sharing repair policy inspired by biological wound healing: local distress signals guide surviving modules toward damaged regions to close fragmented gaps, after which each displaced module locally retraces its own motions to recover the pre-damage shape, using only local information and no absolute position sensing.
